Remove dead flat tree traversal forbidden checks [5/9]

Design doc: go/fixing-slot-assignment-recalc

Before two-pass slot assignment recalc, flat tree traversal was
forbidden for most of a recalc, including while its side effects ran,
so several consumers that can be reached from those side effects
(display locks, accessibility, serialization, SVG resources) checked
SlotAssignmentRecalcInProgressScope::IsFlatTreeTraversalForbidden() and
skipped their work.

Flat tree traversal is now only forbidden during pass 1, which computes
assignment data and runs none of that code. The checks are dead, so
this CL removes them. The remaining callers (FlatTreeTraversal,
HTMLSlotElement, and Node::AssignedSlot()) report using the lazy flat
tree entry points while traversal is forbidden (see
SlotAssignmentRecalcInProgressScope).

This also removes
DisplayLockContextTest.WillStartLifecycleUpdatePreservesPendingTopLayerCheckWhenDeferred,
which called WillStartLifecycleUpdate() while flat tree traversal was
forbidden to exercise the removed check in
DisplayLockContext::DetermineIfSubtreeHasTopLayerElement(). That state
can no longer occur.

[remoting] Add PerfettoTraceConfig

Adds `PerfettoTraceConfig` (`perfetto_trace_config.{h,cc}`) to
`//remoting/base:perfetto_tracing` so standalone Chrome Remote Desktop
benchmarks and multi-process Me2Me trace producers share a single
in-memory Perfetto `TraceConfig` builder:

- Configures in-memory Perfetto session buffers (`RING_BUFFER` by
  default or `DISCARD` for offline CLI benchmarks) with
  `BUILTIN_CLOCK_MONOTONIC` and periodic incremental state clearing
  (`clear_period_ms = 500`) so ring-buffer wrap-around and lookback
  trimming retain valid `InternedData` and `TrackDescriptor` tables.
- Parses `TrackEventConfig` category patterns and `-category`
  exclusions while disabling unlisted categories via
  `disabled_categories("*")` and keeping `"__metadata"` and thread-time
  sampling enabled.
- Provides `IsValid()` to check buffer size bounds (`1 KiB..64 MiB`),
  non-negative duration, and at least one positive category entry.

Test: remoting_unittests --gtest_filter=PerfettoTraceConfigTest.*

[WebApps] Reset banner pipeline data on installability recheck

RecheckInstallabilityForLoadedPage() restarts the banner pipeline on the
same page, but kept the data of the previous run. If a page swaps a
Play-app manifest for a web-app manifest, the stale native Java app data
made CreateAddToHomescreenParams() hit a CHECK and crash the browser.

- Clear the pipeline data on every recheck, not only on navigation.
- Store the native Java app data only after the native check succeeds.
- Also drop the ambient badge, the pending prompt event, and the old
  callbacks when a recheck runs in the COMPLETE state (WebAPK, TWA, or
  desktop uninstall).

[remoting] Add DeleteUntrustedEntrySafely() for Windows host setup

Add remoting/host/installer/win/setup/secure_delete.{h,cc} with
DeleteUntrustedEntrySafely(), which removes an untrusted file, reparse
point, or directory subtree from the host configuration directory
during setup. It is used by SecureConfigDirectory() in the follow-up
CL.

- All checks and modifications for an entry are done on a single handle
  opened via OpenEntryNoFollow(), verified against its expected
  canonical path before any modification.
- Non-reparse subdirectories are held open without FILE_SHARE_WRITE /
  FILE_SHARE_DELETE and locked to an admin-only DACL across child
  enumeration; files (including hard links) are unlinked without
  modifying their security descriptor.
- Entries are unlinked via FileDispositionInfoEx POSIX semantics, using
  extended-length paths so trailing-dot and long-path names are handled,
  and deletion fails closed if an entry cannot be safely unlinked or
  exceeds the recursion depth limit.

[Direct Offers] Persist pass_view_url for Wallet loyalty cards

Google Wallet sends a per-pass `pass_view_url` in
`AutofillValuableSpecifics` for loyalty cards, but Chrome previously
dropped it when converting the specifics into a `LoyaltyCard`. This CL
persists it so that the upcoming "Offers and loyalty cards" settings
leaf can link each loyalty card to its pass in Google Wallet.

- Adds `LoyaltyCard::pass_view_url()`.
- Converts `pass_view_url` in both directions in valuables_sync_util.
  Invalid or non-Google URLs are dropped (via
  `IsValidWalletManagementUrl()`) without rejecting the loyalty card,
  so the field remains optional.
- Extends `HasEmptyOrValidManagementUrl()` in ValuableSyncBridge to
  loyalty cards. Like Autofill AI Wallet passes, loyalty cards with an
  invalid `pass_view_url` are rejected when
  kAutofillAiWalletServerProvidedDeepLink is enabled.
- Renames Autofill.Ai.ValuableSync.ManagementUrlStatus{EntityType} to
  Autofill.ValuableSync.ManagementUrlStatus{ValuableType}, since
  it now also covers loyalty cards. Loyalty cards are recorded to the
  aggregate and to the new .LoyaltyCard variant.
- Moves the shared insert code of ValuablesTable::SetLoyaltyCards() and
  AddOrUpdateLoyaltyCard() into InsertOrReplaceLoyaltyCard().
- Adds a `pass_view_url` column to the `loyalty_cards` table
  (WebDatabase version 163). Existing loyalty cards keep an empty URL
  until the next sync.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAMS=Autofill.Ai.ValuableSync.ManagementUrlStatus{EntityType} is replaced by Autofill.ValuableSync.ManagementUrlStatus{ValuableType}.

[contextual-tasks] Extend contextual tasks context flag expiry to M170

Trunk is at M160, so `contextual-tasks-context` and
`contextual-tasks-context-library` (expiry M159) are already past
expiry, and `contextual-tasks-context-multi-turn-tab-relevance` (expiry
M160) expires once trunk moves to M161. This CL sets the
`expiry_milestone` of all three flags in
`chrome/browser/flag-metadata.json` to 170. This keeps the flags from
being hidden or deleted from `chrome://flags` as expired.

[crubit] Explicitly enable `impl_trait_in_assoc_type` for `cc_std`.

Crubit's hand-written `support/cc_std_impl/string.rs` relies on
`#![feature(impl_trait_in_assoc_type)]`, but `rs_bindings_from_cc` does
not always emit this feature-enabling snippet in the generated `.rs`
file.  Before this CL this problem broke the build of `cc_std` in some
build configurations (see the linked bug).

This CL works around the problem by:

* adding an internal `unstable_rust_features` parameter to
  the `rust_api_from_cpp` GN template
* and then using this new, internal parameter from the `cc_std` target
  to enable the `impl_trait_in_assoc_type` feature

2a84ef4ae68443dc4d42ce84dd1bd97b26f0c858 [ diff ] 
[HLS] Fix live stream failing to resume after preload

SegmentStream::highest_segment_index_ was not cleared by
ResetExpectingFutureManifest(), so after a reset every segment in the
re-fetched live playlist was dropped as old and the rendition quit with
"Segment queue never had any data". Track the reset with
awaiting_manifest_after_reset_ and start from the beginning of the new
playlist until it is consumed.

Also make ResumeLivePlayback remove all buffered data and reset the
parser state, so a stale buffered tail cannot collide with re-fetched
segments.

Traffic annotations: fix random reviewer selection command

The previous tail -n+10 command in TRAFFIC_ANNOTATION_OWNERS relied
on a hardcoded line offset and broke when lines above or below the
CET section changed, allowing for comment lines, blank lines, or the
temporary bulk-approval owner to be selected.

This CL moves the temporary bulk-approval owner above the CET section
and replaces the command with a combination of standard Linux CLI tool
uses (heading-bounded sed, grep, and shuf -n 1) that selects only from
non-comment, non-empty lines within the CET section, with the section
ending with the next empty line.

tree
5754d2718f4f42ad19db867bc58cdac9330b82a0
parent
05457c2e19eb1fccdd05f2a43aec1797c0bb2883 [ diff ] 
[masonry] Complete overlap-join support

Grid-axis `overlap-join` insets in `grid-lanes` previously resolved the
crossing rule width to zero, stopping rules at the crossing gutter
center instead of extending to the far edge of the crossing rule.

When the crossing rule-width property has a single value, store that
width directly to avoid allocating a per-gap width list and looking up
each intersection's crossing gap.

When `overlap-join` is combined with a multi-value or repeated crossing
rule-width list, `grid-lanes` resolves crossing widths per lane while
skipping artificial boundaries. At non-cap `grid-lanes` main-gap
junction endpoints with a multi-value crossing width list, binary-search
each adjacent lane's sorted cross-gap slice. A main-gap intersection
stores only a stacking-axis offset rather than cross-gap indices, so the
binary search finds the crossing gutters at that offset (or across its
overlap window) without scanning the lane from the start.

[TabModel] Short-circuit getPendingRewindTab when no closures pending

Previously, RewoundList#getPendingRewindTab linearly scanned
mRewoundTabs on every tab ID miss even when no tab closures were
pending. When TabModelSelectorBase#getModelForTabId is called for
unknown IDs, this caused O(N_tabs) scans per lookup.

Short-circuit getPendingRewindTab with !hasPendingClosures() so misses
return in O(1) when there are no pending closures. Also update
PendingTabClosureManagerTest's FakeTabModel to implement getTabById and
fix the two partial-cancel assertions that previously passed
ignoreClosing=false on already-restored tabs.

e9c5c1c4da2ad10c308ad46bdc0d6286c8398881
parent
f55c7710260faed945574cbf877005a6a05b94c2 [ diff ] 
Composite scrollbar layers needing mask filter under ancestor mask filter

PaintedScrollbarLayerImpl::AppendThumbQuads sets a pill rounded-corner
gfx::MaskFilterInfo on the thumb's SharedQuadState when Fluent
scrollbars are enabled. Because viz::SharedQuadState holds only a single
MaskFilterInfo, if the scrollbar layer is also subject to an ancestor
fast rounded-corner clip (or other EffectNode MaskFilterInfo), setting
the thumb's MaskFilterInfo would overwrite the ancestor mask filter.

Instead of dropping one of the mask filters, expose
cc::Layer::AppliesMaskFilterInfo() (overridden by PaintedScrollbarLayer
when using a solid-color thumb outside web tests) and detect in
PropertyTreeManager::SwitchToEffectNodeWithSynthesizedClip when such a
layer is subject to an active EffectNode MaskFilterInfo above it. When
both apply, composite the scrollbar layer into its own render pass
(RenderSurfaceReason::kRoundedCorner) so that it has an empty inherited
MaskFilterInfo within its own render pass and can apply its thumb
MaskFilterInfo while its render pass is still filtered by the parent
render pass. Also CHECK in PaintedScrollbarLayerImpl::AppendThumbQuads
that shared_quad_state->mask_filter_info is empty before setting the
thumb's MaskFilterInfo.

[omnibox] Match Views' keyword placeholder color and screen reader text

In the full WebUI omnibox popup:
- A keyword's placeholder (e.g. "Enter a word or two" for @history) now
  uses the Views omnibox's dim placeholder color, like the AIM hint,
  instead of a fixed gray.
- Screen readers now get the Views omnibox's accessible name for the
  input ("Address and search bar") and, while the input has no
  placeholder text, the usage hint that the Views omnibox adds to its
  accessible placeholder ("Press up or down arrow for auto completions.
  Type to autocomplete.").

While the input shows a placeholder (the AIM hint or a keyword's),
screen readers still get just that text, with the AIM hint's tab
symbol. In Views they get it without the symbol, followed by the usage
hint.

9f57744b4508eda2597eaf9d23397bf6ace6c3dd [ diff ] 
[chromecast] Harvest child-process UMA histograms

In out-of-process Cast Web Runtime builds (such as Android TV
mediashell where :cast_browser_process, :privileged_process0, and
:sandboxed_process0 run in separate OS processes), UMA histograms
recorded in the GPU and renderer child processes were never harvested:

- CastBrowserMainParts never initialized a GlobalHistogramAllocator, so
  BrowserChildProcessHostImpl::CreateMetricsAllocator() and
  RenderProcessHostImpl::CreateMetricsAllocator() returned early
  without allocating shared-memory PersistentHistogramAllocator
  segments for child processes.
- CastBrowserMainParts never initialized
  metrics::SubprocessMetricsProvider::CreateInstance().
- CastRuntimeHistogramSnapshotManager::GetDeltas() did not call
  base::StatisticsRecorder::ImportProvidedHistogramsSync() before
  snapshotting deltas, and passed include_persistent=false to
  PrepareDeltas().

Initialize local-memory persistent histograms via
InstantiatePersistentHistograms() and SubprocessMetricsProvider in
CastBrowserMainParts::PreCreateThreads(), and import provided
histograms with include_persistent=true in
CastRuntimeHistogramSnapshotManager so GPU and renderer histograms are
delivered to Cast Core over gRPC.

Bug: b:577236732, b:560033352
Test: cast_cast_core_unittests --gtest_filter=*HistogramFlattener*

[ttc] Add keyboard shortcut prefs

Adds two string profile prefs to //chrome/browser/ttc/core:prefs,
registered on every platform so that every TTC UI can share them:
- ttc.keyboard_shortcut (default "Alt+Shift+K"): the shortcut that
  starts or ends a TTC session.
- ttc.hold_to_speak_shortcut (default "Alt+Shift+Space"): the shortcut
  that TTC listens to while it is held down.

They use the format of ui::Command::StringToAccelerator(), like
browser.voice_typing_hotkey, so desktop can read them. An empty value
means the user cleared the shortcut. Neither Chrome on Android, desktop
Chrome nor ChromeOS uses the defaults. Nothing handles the shortcuts
yet.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[Actor] Skip surface lookup when no task is active
📋 Metadata
commit
b040a997e5c9007f9c4e7dfc0a107243ac2d4d8f
[ log ]
author
Fred Mello <fredmello@google.com>
Fri Nov 20 20:21:57 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 20 20:21:57 2026
tree
9b210b157464b937d8d43368678067786ad682f1
parent
b48bb59bd8e62cf3b478c1329cd3dcca09de0ce9 [ diff ] 
[Actor] Skip surface lookup when no task is active

ActorKeyedService::GetActingActorTaskForWebContents() maps the
WebContents to an ActorSurfaceHandle before it looks at the tasks.
ActorSurfaceHandle::From() is linear in the number of live surfaces
(roughly the number of open tabs), and callers such as
ChromeNavigationUIData run this function during navigations, so they
pay for the lookup even when the profile has no actor task.

Return early when there are no active tasks. The loop over the tasks
would find nothing in that case, so behavior is unchanged. Add a unit
test for the function, which had none.

Options not taken:
- Have callers check GetActiveTasksCount() first. Every caller,
  including each new one, would have to remember to do it.
- Look the surface up in the profile's ActorSurfaceRegistry, which
  already indexes tabs. It stops resolving a tab as soon as the tab's
  surface is destroyed, while From() keeps the mapping until a posted
  task clears it, so the lookup result would change.
- Make ActorSurfaceHandle::From() cheaper. Its scan is linear by
  design, to avoid keeping a second index in sync.

Bug: b:585636202
Change-Id: Ie40e1fd210ce22f3540e3f771962f154bf046e38
Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8842919

f016466bf83810dd354ef2799b6c3fabb13d0192 [ diff ] 
Disable VariationsService HTTP fallback by default

VariationsService currently retries failed HTTPS seed fetches over HTTP.
Gate this fallback behind a new kVariationsHttpFallback feature
(disabled by default) so failed HTTPS variations fetches no longer fall
back to HTTP unless explicitly enabled.

Note: Since the default state is off, it means only an HTTPS seed could
re-enable it.

[Vertical Tabs] Share the ESC drag-cancel handler with the tab switcher
📋 Metadata
commit
c11f762cfbfec56bc990e3a3b29f55f4372ab8c0
[ log ]
author
Gazal <agazal@google.com>
Fri Nov 20 20:19:18 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 20 20:19:18 2026
tree
fd1ba1a75930c48fe89072e708eb0a24e574049c
parent
95852770cbd80099e3e6d631f1f3df171c3dfd3f [ diff ] 
[Vertical Tabs] Share the ESC drag-cancel handler with the tab switcher

Only one back press handler can cancel tab drags. On tablets that can
show Vertical Tabs, VerticalTabListCoordinator registered its own
handler group at startup, so the tab switcher's group never registered.
Pressing ESC during a tab switcher drag hid the tab switcher instead of
cancelling the drag.

The list now uses ChromeTabbedActivity's shared group, which the tab
switcher already uses. It registers the group only if nobody has yet,
and no longer removes it on destroy, because its drag handler already
leaves the group when destroyed. ESC now cancels drags in both.

This is a prep step for building the Vertical Tabs list only when it is
first shown, to cut startup ANRs and main-thread work for tablet users
who never turn it on. Once the list is built late, the tab switcher can
register first.

ca03268055374d1557546bbbcf5d439416b22884 [ diff ] 
Add missing commas in WebSocketExtensionParserTest.InvalidPatterns

Three adjacent string literals in `InvalidPatterns` were missing
trailing commas, causing C++ to concatenate four invalid test cases into
a single string (`"foo; b\xFFr=bazfoo; bar=b\xFF..."`). As a result, the
parser failed on the first invalid parameter name and never exercised
8-bit characters in unquoted or quoted parameter values or a trailing
backslash individually.

Also include the failing pattern in the `EXPECT_EQ` failure message.

[a11y] Skip redundant DOM traversal when detaching layout subtrees
📋 Metadata
commit
33457851dc89c770fb3bffc0fe298bc6560ec52e
[ log ]
author
Lucas Radaelli <lucasradaelli@google.com>
Fri Nov 20 20:15:26 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 20 20:15:26 2026
tree
2edb4d2b038251bc0f33c39742891ceb5b1aa7a6
parent
27de69f2b8aa818a3040a8bbcc303f16822466b3 [ diff ] 
[a11y] Skip redundant DOM traversal when detaching layout subtrees

This change reduces Blink accessibility CPU time by 11.24% and overall
renderer main thread (`CrRendererMain`) CPU time by 1.76% in Speedometer
3. Specifically, it reduces CPU time in
`AXObjectCacheImpl::RemoveSubtreeInternal` by 43.06%.

Previously, detaching a layout subtree walked all descendant DOM nodes
for every ancestor in the subtree. Layout detachment already processes
child nodes before their parent node. Walking the DOM subtree again
repeated work on already-detached descendants. Now, Blink skips the
redundant DOM child traversal when detaching a container layout tree.
Blink also allocates cycle-tracking sets only when subtree removal
recurses into children. This change does not alter accessibility
behavior.

9118950888dff92fdf4a1f6fb72f0899b9a32b19 [ diff ] 
[discardable_memory] Fail client allocations above the IPC size limit

The mojom AllocateLockedDiscardableSharedMemory() call takes a uint32_t
size, but ClientDiscardableSharedMemoryManager computes the segment size
as a size_t and AllocateOnIO() static_casts it to uint32_t. Rounded
sizes above 4 GiB were silently truncated, so the browser allocated a
much smaller segment (or none), and the allocation only failed later
when the client tried to map the full size.

Return nullptr early when the rounded allocation size doesn't fit in a
uint32_t, and use checked_cast<uint32_t>() in AllocateOnIO() so any
future regression crashes instead of truncating. Make the test
manager enforce the same limit, and add a test for a 4 GiB + 1 page
allocation.

Bug: None

Add allow_fake_dmserver build flag

This lets first-party developers turn on --use-fake-dmserver in local,
Chrome-branded builds. It also lets downstream vendors turn it off
easier.

The --use-fake-dmserver switch was gated by GOOGLE_CHROME_BRANDING.

Add a dedicated allow_fake_dmserver GN arg, and use it for the existing
--use-fake-dmserver code. The arg defaults to
is_linux && !is_chrome_branded, because fake_dmserver supports only
desktop Linux. Non-branded builds on other platforms lose the switch.

1af36d57495821615145d58a5b0a0b8438bf3aa9
parent
2947992c1706daeeab677a19f97c5d64e74b364c [ diff ] 
[media_router] Drop unreadable routes from third-party providers

Chrome binds to third-party MediaRouteProviderServices. Some send route
descriptors with Parcelable classes that only exist in their own APK,
such as a custom IntentFilter subclass. AndroidX MediaRouter reads them
on the main thread without catching BadParcelableException, which
crashes the browser.

Pass MediaRouter a ContextWrapper that proxies the provider service
Messenger and reads the route fields MediaRouter uses before it does.
Routes that fail to unparcel are dropped. Other fields, such as the
extras that hold CastDevice, stay packed.

[Data Controls] Fix find bar paste crash when its page goes away

The find bar checked its tab's page before reading the clipboard source,
but that read is asynchronous. If the tab closed or navigated to another
document in the meantime, the paste verdict looked up the rules service
for a null profile and crashed.

Check the destination again once the clipboard source is known, and
don't restore replaced data when its page is gone, so the find bar
pastes the OS clipboard text. Add unit tests that hold the clipboard
read while the tab is closed or navigated.

tree
b38f65452a40ffbaae55f214f7ab4fefd704c259
parent
b23d6bf1c3b63da094866a90c45745343da19e6d [ diff ] 
[Tab] Notify observers when a detached tab regains its activity

ReparentingTask#detach() detaches the tab and notifies
onActivityAttachmentChanged(tab, null). If starting the new activity
fails, ReparentingTask#finishAsNoOp() re-attaches the tab to its
original window with a null TabDelegateFactory. TabImpl#attachToWindow()
only notified observers when a delegate factory was supplied, so
observers never learned the tab was attached again and kept the state
they tore down on detach.

Also notify onActivityAttachmentChanged(tab, window) when a detached tab
is attached to a window that has an activity. A window-only swap on an
attached tab, and attaching a detached tab to a window without an
activity, still do not notify.

This does not make finishAsNoOp() a full rollback: state that observers
drop for good on detach, such as the activity held by
ActivityTabWebContentsDelegateAndroid, is not restored.

Translator API: unload idle TranslateKit models and record lifecycle histograms

Add TranslationAPIModelIdleUnloadTimeout (1 minute on Android, disabled
elsewhere). On idle timeout or base::MemoryConsumer pressure,
TranslateKitClient frees the underlying TranslateKit translator (and
purges free heap pages on Android), reloading it on the next call.

Record AI.Translation.ModelUnloadReason when TranslateKitClient unloads
a loaded model on idle timeout or memory pressure, plus
AI.Translation.ModelReloadSuccess and AI.Translation.ModelReloadTime
when an unloaded translator reloads its model on a later call.

32f6b5f380bd1da01e63e51f0be1314233d16fdc

tree
66c37b350e883a0312b74e133519c3a0bcfb976e
parent
dad0223d8bd7ca588975a2ef8ddae0c2cb34e415 [ diff ] 
Make External Navigation redirect clobbers renderer initiated

For complicated historical reasons External Navigation doesn't support
modifying the URL for a navigation or triggering a redirect, so when we
get a navigation to an intent URL that targets the current browser we
load the target URL as a new top level navigation in the current tab
instead of performing a redirect.

However, when this new top level navigation results from a redirect, we
lose the fact that this is a redirect, and so we get behavior like
updating the URL being displayed to the user before this redirect
commits, which allows a website to redirect to an arbitrary URL and
have that URL show in the URL bar before it gets committed and the
previous page unloads.

If a malicious triggering page can find a way to prevent the redirect
from committing and stay as a pending navigation, this arbitrary URL
can be displayed over the malicious page indefinitely creating a very
convincing spoof.

In order to avoid this URL-updating behavior, we can mark new top level
navigations resulting from redirects as renderer-initiated so the URL
doesn't get trusted as though it's coming from a user.

tree
7e3fd324d4aca2f6c6547b95888f6eb758adbebd
parent
41a941c290b60527d3685eb7cbd4f217a313d626 [ diff ] 
NewTabPageUI: Lazily pin warm New Tab Page renderer per profile

This CL adds NtpRendererKeepAlive behind kKeepNtpRendererAlive to
lazily hold a pending-reuse refcount on the last committed 1P WebUI NTP
renderer per profile on desktop. It skips pinning when policy, an
extension, or a 3P DSE overrides the NTP or when explicit/OS process
caps are reached at commit time, and relies on moderate+ memory
pressure (plus process exit, profile teardown, and override changes) to
bound RAM and multi-profile retention.

Opening a new tab or window otherwise pays cold renderer startup once
the previous NTP navigates away and exits. Keeping the warm NTP
renderer pinned avoids process creation and WebUI re-initialization
without affecting startup before the first NTP load.

Standalone Linux Release (N=32):
- Ctrl+T: MainUI -59.5 ms (-23.3%), FCP2 -58.5 ms (-19.9%),
  WebUINTP -85.0 ms (-22.7%).
- Ctrl+N: MainUI -58.0 ms (-22.1%), FCP2 -58.5 ms (-18.8%),
  WebUINTP -81.5 ms (-20.8%); BrowserWindow.FirstPaint P50 neutral.

2bba018eefc5304bc02a0c3f8894883fcdb8bdd3
parent
ce5bfb545d47341170eb553d7edf3f9676da8a52 [ diff ] 
[Android] Record where pointer-down events come from

UMA cannot tell a touch that came through an input device from one
that a tool injected. Add Android.Input.PointerDownProvenance: one enum
sample per ACTION_DOWN delivered to a ChromeBaseAppCompatActivity
window, built from two facts on the event:

- whether it has an input device, i.e. a non-negative device ID.
  Events from adb shell input and UiAutomation have none.
- whether Android flagged it as made by, or passed through, an
  accessibility service (MotionEvent flag 0x800, set from Android 12).

The four combinations are separate buckets. A single accessibility
bucket would mix a person's touches with a service's gestures: on
Android 12 a touch carries the flag whenever a service that can
perform gestures is enabled, and only the device ID tells the two
apart. Android 11 sets no flag and drops the device ID of touches made
while an accessibility feature filters input; the histogram summary
says so. Observed on Android 11, 12 and 15 emulators.

The check is in PointerDownProvenanceRecorder, a package-private class
beside the activity, so the base activity gains only the
dispatchTouchEvent() override. No existing hook sees every pointer-down
in every Chrome activity: EventForwarder sees web content only, and
the window callback proxy in ApplicationStatus is shared with other
embedders and sits above the touch filters of custom tabs and first
run.

There is no feature flag, so the histogram cannot be turned off
remotely: the recorder adds one sample and cannot change dispatch. A
CachedFlag could gate it, including for touches that arrive before
native code loads, with a one-launch lag. Two buckets (device or not)
would be smaller, but could not tell an accessibility service's
gestures from adb.

809b1f3da9ec786ad11d03ea2f063b838e5925a3 [ diff ] 
[remoting] Implement Terminate & Reconnect in UserDesktopSessionBackend

When Curtain Mode is required and the user only has a local graphical
session, UserDesktopSessionBackend now decides the outcome: it reports
UNCURTAINED_SESSION in custom mode, SESSION_REJECTED for a user who is
ineligible in auto-session mode, and LOCAL_SESSION_RUNNING for an
eligible user. If Terminate-Local-Session is set for an eligible user,
it terminates the selected local session via systemd-logind, waits for
SessionRemoved (30s timeout, OPERATION_TIMEOUT), and requests a remote
user session.

DesktopSessionLinux takes its construction arguments as an Options
struct and exposes ValidateSessionAccess(), so the backend can run the
credential/PAM checks before reporting a curtain error.

  tab groups via areAnyTabsPartOfSharedGroup and ungroup
  already-grouped tabs across multi-tab selections before creating
  the new group.
- Update unit tests in TabGroupUtilsUnitTest,
  TabGroupListBottomSheetMediatorUnitTest,
  TabGroupMenuActionHandlerUnitTest,
  TabContextMenuCoordinatorUnitTest, and
  TabGridContextMenuCoordinatorUnitTest.

parent
f7f081e7ca28cf83827f6a81e84c8dc7ef210dd7 [ diff ] 
[Vertical Tabs] Move rail collapse controller to the Side UI container

The rail collapse state (expanded, collapsed, or forced collapsed in a
narrow window) decides how wide the rail is.
VerticalTabsSideUiCoordinator owns the rail's sizing and answers Side
UI's width queries from that state, but the controller lived inside
VerticalTabListCoordinator. Now VerticalTabsSideUiCoordinator creates
the controller and builds the list through a factory that receives it.
There is no behavior change, because the factory is still called right
away.

This lets a follow-up build the list only when Vertical Tabs is first
shown, which removes startup ANRs and main-thread work for tablet users
who never turn it on.

Translator API: keep the translation service when a language pack lands

Send updated OnDeviceTranslationServiceConfig messages to a running
utility process when new language packs install, appending packs so
existing virtual path indices stay valid for live Translators. Same-pair
updates wait for the next launch, and pack removals reset the service.

parent
80eca535086bfb6ffa557bdb5daec5de101a08b6 [ diff ] 
[GEiC] Document where provider-specific Glic flags go

Provider-dependent feature flags should live in `provider_features.h`,
which only `GlicProviderSpec`, tests, and flag registration can include.
A new `GlicFeature` value whose flag goes into a separate features file
lets production code read the flag directly instead of going through the
spec.

* List the steps for adding a provider-specific flag at the top of
  `features.h`, where new Glic flags usually go.
* Remind authors at the top of each `SupportsFeature()` and on
  `GlicFeature`. A lint isn't used because not every `GlicFeature` has a
  flag.

2788f434b6b60f14e1b2836ab34fa1a730fc399f
parent
ed8e8a03e5b03604d141d0820cb1b380857594ae [ diff ] 
[base/android] Merge SelfCompactionState and RunningCompactionState

The two CompactionState subclasses were identical apart from their
histogram prefix: both used a 100 MiB per-task cap and a 30 second
delay after PreFreeze tasks, and each spelled its prefix out twice.

Make CompactionState a concrete class that takes the metric prefix,
replace the virtual GetDelayAfterPreFreezeTasks() with a constant, and
replace the templated OnTriggerCompact<State>() with an overload that
takes the prefix. Also remove the friend declarations for the deleted
classes, which never applied to the anonymous-namespace classes anyway.

No behavior change.

Fri Nov 20 19:40:19 2026
tree
15490e71bbcb696ca5445f6dd7515209b7192871
parent
bc56367b8326200a14e7152a0a78acd22b482c07 [ diff ] 
[macOS] Exit Chrome-launched app shim cleanly if Chrome exits

When Chrome launches an app shim (with kLaunchedByChromeProcessId)
shortly before Chrome quits or restarts, such as for background
notification handling, the launching Chrome process can exit before the
shim connects. Previously this triggered LOG(FATAL) crashes in
AppShimController::FindOrLaunchChrome() and PollForChromeReady().

Exit cleanly via [NSApp terminate:nil] with a warning instead when the
launching Chrome process has already exited or terminated. Use
sysctl(KERN_PROC_PID) to detect gone, zombie, or exiting Chrome
processes both when AppKit fails to find the PID (preserving LOG(FATAL)
for live-PID lookup failures from crbug.com/40261534 and non-positive
PIDs) and when NSRunningApplication.terminated has not yet updated.
User-launched and shim-launched Chrome startup failures remain fatal.

[actor] Implement GetScreenshotTool

screenshot of the viewport via PageContextFetcher and return a
ScreenshotToolResult, failing if the target tab goes away, crashes, or
navigates away from the page.

migrated from the existing tool implementation in ttc::ToolController,
which will be removed in a follow-up CL.

Add order-independent OriginMatcher::operator==

Store rules in a sorted, deduplicated base::flat_set with
MatchAllOriginsRule ordered first so wildcard matchers short-circuit on
the first rule and OriginMatcher::operator== compares rules in O(N) time
without serializing.

[iOS][Gemini] Exit fullscreen when opening Gemini container

Exit browser fullscreen mode whenever the Gemini container is opened:
- Exit fullscreen right before initial container presentation once any
  delayed auto-submit page context generation finishes.
- Exit fullscreen when re-invoking an already-presented container right before expanding it.

45b10021fb4a1a5f5c09397ec6581c4ee78385c7

tree
83d8618d20dfec524595378b338f5013f9e7343f
parent
cb392d0a7325c39a28e360fd40542ee56e7e51c1 [ diff ] 
[ttc] Record TTC sessions while the debug tools are enabled

Connects the TTC pipeline to TtcDebugToolsService, so every TTC session
is recorded into a TtcDebugSession while the TTC debug tools are on.

SessionControllerImpl begins the session's record in its constructor
and ends it in its destructor, while the session journal that the
record logs to still exists. The record stays in TtcDebugToolsService
after the session, for review. It is itself the DebugSink that the
pipeline reports to: GetDebugSink() returns it, so the MES client and
the audio controller report straight into it.

SessionControllerImpl also reports what only the browser side knows
about: session errors and page context fetch failures. The new
TtcDebugSession::OnPageContextFetchFailed() records a failed fetch
once: as a blocked page if the page may not share its context, and as
a failure otherwise.

The record is only begun when TtcDebugToolsService exists for the
profile, which requires both TTC and the debug tools feature. Otherwise
GetDebugSink() stays null and nothing in the pipeline reports anything,
so TTC behaves exactly as before.

The debug tools are only built on Desktop and Desktop Android: a new
enable_ttc_debug_tools GN arg (ENABLE_TTC_DEBUG_TOOLS) defaults to
!is_android || is_desktop_android. chrome://ttc-internals is a Chrome
Desktop tool, used side by side with the tab being debugged, which
phones can't do. Building it there would only add size to the phone
binary for something that can't be used.

TtcKeyedServiceFactory now depends on TtcDebugToolsServiceFactory. This
builds the debug tools factory at startup with the other keyed service
factories, and makes sure the debug tools service and its record
outlive the sessions that report to them.

Tracing tool calls through the browser to their effect comes in a
follow-up.

Tests:
- TtcSessionControllerImplTest: no sink with the debug tools off; with
  them on, the record is the sink, session errors reach it, and it ends
  with the session and logs its open turn to the session journal.
- TtcDebugSessionTest: blocked and failed page context.
- TtcDebugPipelineBrowserTest: the real pipeline (conversation, MES
  client, audio) reports into the record, server errors become
  problems, a tool call with malformed arguments is recorded with its
  error response, and nothing is created with either flag off.
  SendServerAudio() moves from HotlProtocolBrowserTest to
  AppBrowserTestBase so that this test can use it too.

Part 11 of the chrome://ttc-internals series. Design: go/ttc-internals

tree
75fef1dd119cdd606b7170d05037acf493bd226a
parent
d7f11fa3b24d5d17f35286f2ca399ff0479f14bf [ diff ] 
[TabUI Virtualization] Handle TabModels joining or leaving in place

When the host's list of TabModels changed, TabListDataProvider detached
from every model, cleared its items without telling observers, and
waited for the next data reset. Now, when at least one model stays, it
only detaches the models that left and removes their cards, and attaches
the models that joined and adds their tabs, with normal insert and
remove events. With no model in common it still switches as before.

Follow-ups:
- Add a TabModel's tabs when it finishes loading.
- Decide which TabModel drives the highlight, and move the highlight
  when that model changes. Until then, the first model leaving doesn't
  move the highlight until the next reset.
- Look tabs up through the provider in TabListMediator, so cards from
  models other than the host's show.

[iOS][Gardening] Suppress testCloseLastTabInIncognito on iPad Simulator

`SharedTabGroupsTestCase/testCloseLastTabInIncognito` is flaking and
failing retries on iOS 26+ iPad Simulators (`ipad-simulator`,
`ios27-beta-simulator`, `ios27-sdk-simulator`) because
`grey_longPress()` on `ShowTabsButton()` runs immediately after tapping
`TabGridDoneButton()` without waiting for the Tab Grid exit transition
to idle, intermittently firing a tap instead of opening the context
menu.

Scope the `[ Pass Failure ]` expectation to
`[ iOS26 iOS27 iPad Simulator ]` so iOS 18.5 iPad Simulator coverage
remains enforced.

[GEiC] Show the Glic provider switch notice

Shows the provider switch notice from crrev.com/c/8819025 when the user
opens Glic after an admin switched their provider.

This mimics the pending provider change relaunch panel
(crrev.com/c/8747524): each Host decides once whether a notice is due,
and both the NoWebview and webview panels reuse the relaunch panel with
a "Continue" button. The relaunch prompt takes priority.

Continue records the current provider as known, so the notice doesn't
come back. A follow-up will listen for that pref and update all open
panels.

Gated by kGeicProviderSwitchNotice, disabled by default. Strings aren't
translated yet.

cf85689eb4b219c996f542c6b0ddb72775238c57
parent
c73038e28b9c4b01999b35e6f71c7c0111604353 [ diff ] 
[Tab] Rebind TabViewManager margins when a tab changes window

TabViewManagerImpl created its BrowserControlsMarginAdapter lazily and
never replaced it, so it stayed bound to the browser controls of the
first activity the tab was shown in. After the tab was reparented,
custom tab views such as the sad tab were laid out with the old
activity's browser controls margins.

Destroy the adapter when the tab is detached from its window, and do not
create one while the tab is detached. When the tab is attached to a
window, recreate the adapter against that window if a custom view is
showing. The margins observer is now registered once in the constructor
so that recreating the adapter does not register it again.

Look up the browser controls through
BrowserControlsStateProviderSupplier on the tab's window instead of the
deprecated ChromeActivity#getBrowserControlsManager(). ChromeActivity
attaches the same BrowserControlsManager there, and it lets the test
avoid mocking an activity.

0a64dd8d415d98ef374f0e53988bb79ab7eb17b8 [ diff ] 
[ios] Wait for ScreenTime in the ios/web_view test suites

On iOS 27 simulators, a test suite that creates web views right after
the simulator boots can see first loads hang: ScreenTime XPC calls
starve the thread pool that WebKit needs to launch its web content
process. https://crrev.com/c/8590821 fixed this for suites that use
web::WebTestSuite, but ios_web_view_unittests and ios_web_view_inttests
use their own runners and do not get the wait.

This moves the wait into //ios/testing:screen_time_util and calls it
once per suite from WebTestSuite and from both ios/web_view runners.
ios_web_view_inttests gets its own runner instead of
//base/test:run_all_unittests so it can do this.

Without the wait, about 1 in 700 first loads of about:blank in
ios_web_view_unittests hung for 10 s on iOS 27 simulators that were
booting other simulators at the same time, with no navigation callback
at all. The new test flake check on ios-simulator caught this in three
dry runs of a CL that adds web_view tests.

4ff6f83af090d1c5edd472f3baf68e3ed50bbe87
parent
421506b0fc890cd6efea3672728a4e56f61b2696 [ diff ] 
[TabUI Virtualization] Remove tab observers from every observed tab

On hide, destroy and reset, TabListMediator removed its per-tab
observers by walking the host's TabModel. With a multi-model
TabListDataProvider, tabs from other TabModels also get cards and
observers, so those observers stayed attached after the list went away.
The mediator now walks the tabs TabListObserverManager actually
observes. Lists with one TabModel only observe that TabModel's tabs, so
nothing changes for them.

parent
6ecc14a3f43fc060ad74417c07ccfe5d6372ec49 [ diff ] 
[TabUI Virtualization] Find tabs through the provider in TabListMediator

TabListMediator looked up the tab behind each card in the host's
TabModel, so with a multi-model TabListDataProvider, tabs from other
TabModels got no card, and their thumbnail, title, Actor UI and pin
updates were dropped. A new getTabById() in the mediator asks the
provider, which searches every TabModel it observes, and falls back to
the host's TabModel when there is no provider. Card creation and these
updates now use it. Every provider still has one TabModel today, so
nothing changes for existing lists.

tree
4b0b36b5574cc6b2a7977fb98e693c78e19ae329
parent
9fe41c2f99adc66925e597a385815c8ce8e7e388 [ diff ] 
[Fuzzer] Reject solid dictionary growth in UnRAR

In Unpack::Init(), once an unpack window has been allocated by an
earlier entry, subsequent solid entries with a larger dictionary size
(WinSize > AllocWinSize) fatally abort via
UNRAR_FATAL_BAD_ALLOC(WinSize) even when WinSize <= UNPACK_MAX_DICT.

Return a boolean from Unpack::Init() instead of terminating on invalid
or unsupported dictionary sizes, and propagate initialization failures
through CmdExtract and RarReader so RarAnalyzer rejects the archive with
ArchiveAnalysisResult::kFailedDuringIteration.

[NTP] Inset bottom controls by 20px when the LHS is present

With the threads rail enabled, the customize buttons are now 20px (was
16px) from the bottom and inline-end edges of the RHS, centering them
on the settings button at the bottom of the expanded LHS. The
background image attribution is now 20px (was 16px) from the
inline-start edge of the RHS and vertically centered on the customize
buttons (was 16px from the bottom).

This covers the cases where the NTP footer is not shown.

Screenshot: https://screenshot.corp.google.com/0om2emp4bpq5o

[ttc][android] Show TTC in the toolbar shortcut slot

Phones, and windows too narrow for the large-screen toolbar, show the
chosen toolbar shortcut in a single slot next to the address bar. When
TTC is the chosen toolbar shortcut, it now shows there too.

- TtcToolbarButtonController shows the button while the window's
  TtcEntryPoint says TTC sessions can run, and clicking it starts or
  ends a session. TabbedAdaptiveToolbarBehavior registers it when TTC
  is enabled.
- AdaptiveToolbarStatePredictor treats TTC as a valid segment, like
  Glic and Ask Google, so that choosing it in settings takes effect.
  The segmentation platform never ranks TTC, so usage-based prediction
  still never picks it.
- On large screens, the dedicated TTC button already shows the TTC
  shortcut, so the TTC shortcut doesn't also take the optional button
  there.

Fix Windows status tray icon keyboard and screen reader accessibility

Previously, Windows status tray icons used the legacy NOTIFYICONDATA
protocol, which only handled mouse clicks and ignored keyboard or
Narrator activation. Opening a tray context menu from the keyboard also
failed to select the first menu item because MenuController skipped
auto-selection when parent is nullptr.

- Opt StatusIconWin into NOTIFYICON_VERSION_4 (preserving NIF_SHOWTIP
  for OS hover tooltips) and restore the cached tooltip on
  TaskbarCreated.
- Update StatusTrayWin::WndProc to unpack v4 notifications, handle
  NIN_SELECT and NIN_KEYSELECT for primary activation, and pass
  MenuSourceType::kKeyboard for keyboard WM_CONTEXTMENU events.
- Allow MenuController::Run() to auto-select the first item for
  keyboard-opened menus even when parent is nullptr.

Bug: b:526650564
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter="StatusTrayWinTest.*"
Test: views_unittests --gtest_filter="*MenuControllerTest.*WithoutParent*"

385c2b53d641f54e0aaffc5aa49b2edb4fa43536
parent
fb3cf06e42c40144f5e7a31bd46d44824d8bcf32 [ diff ] 
[TabUI Virtualization] Handle tab events with the tab's own TabModel

TabListDataProvider handled new tabs, moves, pin changes and
multi-select state against the first TabModel, so while that model was
still loading, these events from another loaded model were dropped. They
now go to the loaded model that holds the tab. With one model nothing
changes, since every tab is in that model.

Follow-ups:
- Update the list in place when TabModels join or leave.
- Add a TabModel's tabs when it finishes loading.
- Let the host pass the TabModel that drives the highlight, and ignore
  selection changes from the others. That also decides whether a
  background model's current tab, which its model reports as
  multi-selected, shows as multi-selected.
- Look tabs up through the provider in TabListMediator, so cards from
  models other than the host's show.

Skip BlendWithWhite for Highlight when in forced color mode

parent
134d9899a526d327e9099a47d5d2c9534ad0a263 [ diff ] 
Skip BlendWithWhite for Highlight when in forced color mode

Under normal conditions we finish calculating the system `Highlight`
color by using `BlendWithWhite`, which gives it partial transparency
that should render to approximately the original color if rendered on
top of white. In forced color mode, however, we should leave the color
unmodified from the system so that `Highlight` and `HighlightText` have
the expected amount of contrast, regardless of what backdrop they were
painted against.

This change adds a flag to `ColorThemeInfo` to indicate if we are in
forced colors mode, and updates
`LayoutTheme::SystemColorFromColorProvider` to use it to make the
desired distinction.

This also includes a test to validate the assertion that `Highlight`
should always be opaque when in forced colors mode, though ultimately
that is at the discretion of the OS. If this test ever starts to fail
because the OS is providing a non-opaque color there should be an
evaluation to determine if it is better to keep respecting the OS
setting, or if we should force it to opaque in this case.

48952bcc611cf186bb6b65467dd7fba903e8d7d1
parent
fd7235bb5b980710b0eb484da9b4c572b42e4a88 [ diff ] 
[Fullscreen] Don't run a canceled deferred fullscreen enter

A fullscreen request for a tab that is not yet interactable is stored
as an ENTER_FULLSCREEN runnable on the tab and runs when the tab becomes
interactable. Several paths that end the request left the runnable in
place, so the tab could still enter fullscreen later:
- an exit naming a tab the handler does not track returned before
  clearing it;
- an exit-all cleared the tab's display attributes but not its runnable;
- a request from another tab cleared the superseded tab's attributes
  but not its runnable.

Each of these now clears the runnable too. The setter ignores null and
destroyed tabs, whose attributes can no longer be accessed.

b8e5eba3653b4d9f5e665104bf15d7ae78faf497
parent
dc14f43169d9d43226a40791bd4b23a1a3116dcb [ diff ] 
[Fullscreen] Record the fullscreen display on every enter

The popup security check reads TabAttributeKeys.FULLSCREEN_OPTIONS from
the opener's tab to learn which display it is fullscreen on. Until now
the attribute was only written for cross-display requests, so a tab in
fullscreen on its current display reported -1 and lost fullscreen when
it opened a popup on another display.

FullscreenHtmlApiHandlerBase.onEnterFullscreen() now writes the
attribute on every enter, following the branch the handler takes:
- same display (including every request when window move is
  unavailable): the activity's current display;
- cross display: the requested display.
A copy is stored, since the handler marks the caller's options canceled
on parked requests. Browser fullscreen reaches the same method and
also writes it: the security check ignores the display unless native
reports tab fullscreen, and the window really is fullscreen on that
display. If the current display cannot be resolved (below Android
R), the attribute is cleared and the check returns -1 as before.

For same-display requests the handler tracks the tab until it enters
fullscreen, so an exit-all clears a parked or deferred request's tab
even when it is no longer the active tab. Cross-display requests are
not tracked: that handler is about to be destroyed and the tab's
attributes must survive it. When immersive mode hands a fullscreen
session to a newly active tab, the start position moves with it and
the display is resolved again for the window.

The FULLSCREEN_OPTIONS write in the cross-display path is removed; the
start position write stays.

tree
c08e010a378cf19aa9856cdfd9a63a91e105b67f
parent
a91cabde5ea80cd29995251d9919821511a5d652 [ diff ] 
Recalc slot assignment in two passes [4/9]

Design doc: go/fixing-slot-assignment-recalc

The previous CL made a recalc cover every dirty shadow root that its
side effects can read (the "recalc list"), but each root was still
recalculated and had its side effects applied one at a time. A side
effect of the first root (style or layout tree invalidation,
ParentSlotChanged(), RemovedFromFlatTree(), canvas subtree propagation,
accessibility) could read the flat tree through a later root that was
still dirty, getting stale data or starting a nested recalc (rule R6 in
slot_assignment.h).

Recalc now runs in two passes over the recalc list. Pass 1
(SlotAssignment::ComputeAssignment()) computes the assignment data for
every root, with flat tree traversal forbidden and no side effects;
HTMLSlotElement::DidRecalcAssignedNodes() now records its side effects
in a SlotRecalcSideEffects instead of applying them. Pass 2
(ApplySideEffects() and HTMLSlotElement::ApplyRecalcSideEffects())
applies them once every root in the list is clean, so flat tree reads,
including the *NoRecalc accessors, are accurate.

With that, two stopgaps are no longer needed in correct code, and are
replaced by reported fallbacks: the per-root in_recalc_assignment_
bailout becomes a single re-entrancy check in RecalcShadowRoots(), and
Node::AssignedSlot() only takes its slow path if called during pass 1
(previously it did so for the whole recalc).

ListItemOrdinal::ItemUpdated() could now be reached from pass 2 for a
list item that has just left the flat tree, and CHECK-failed
(accessibility/details-summary-crash.html). It now returns early when
there is no enclosing list.

ComputeAssignment() now clears needs_assignment_recalc_ before calling
ManuallyAssignSlots(), so ManuallyAssignSlots() must not dirty slot
assignment, and a CHECK at the end of ComputeAssignment() enforces
that. The ManualSlottingHostElement test helper in
scroll_timeline_test.cc called HTMLSlotElement::Assign(), which does,
and removed host children (a DOM mutation). It now uses
SetManuallyAssignedNodesForRecalc(), like the user-agent hosts, and
unassigns the children instead of removing them, which still detaches
their layout trees.

a52291f1d15946a4f5704b1ed20abe2d6a083064
parent
6d81723f4c9ae585d373fb9403ef8e6b27d61078 [ diff ] 
[FedCM] Separate Android login modal dismissal from sheet dismissal

Previously, `AccountSelectionCoordinator.onActivityResumed()` and
cancelled native-app intents called `onDismissed()`, which intercepted
`mHeaderType == SIGN_IN && mIsModalDialogOpen` to re-show the chooser.
Any genuine dismissal (such as tab navigation) while a login modal was
open would therefore re-show the sheet instead of aborting the request.

Now, `onModalDialogClosedByUser()` handles closing a login CCT or
native-app intent before completion, while `onDismissed()` always
dismisses the request.

parent
19011171db2dea3178c359afa1be97ada8894e3a [ diff ] 
[glic] Short-circuit no-op TabUnderlineController updates

Every open tab observes ActiveTaskContextProvider::OnContextTabsChanged
and DictationKeyedService::AddDictationTabChangedCallback. Previously,
when a tab was not underlined and not in the updated set,
TabUnderlineController still entered UpdateUnderlineView(), allocated
strings and std::list/std::ostringstream buffers for obsolete
crbug-398319435 crash-key logging, and redundantly called
UiDelegate::StopShowing(). Under bulk tab operations (e.g. memory
pressure discarding), this O(K * N) churn caused ChromeOS browser hangs.

This CL:
- Returns early in OnContextTabsChanged() and OnDictationTabChanged()
  when the tab is not underlined by that source and does not need to be.
- Guards HideUnderline() so removing an inactive source is a no-op.
- Records UnderlineSource::kGlic in ShowOrAnimatePinnedUnderline() when
  the underline is already showing from another source.
- Removes the obsolete crbug-398319435 scoped crash-key scaffolding and
  std::list/std::ostringstream reason history from TabUnderlineController.
- Adds TabUnderlineControllerTest unit tests covering no-op updates,
  show/hide transitions, and multi-source underline lifetimes.

974d649b045e0f0dcf3dd02d7a36b7787f24292c [ diff ] 
[Omaha] Move update unit tests into the omaha module

App update check, in-app update, and update metrics unit tests still
compile through a monolithic test target in the top-level Android build
graph even though production update logic resides in its own component.
Keeping those tests in the top-level suite pulls in broad test helper
dependencies and separates update test coverage from the component it
validates.

Relocate the update unit tests into a dedicated module test target
backed by modularized dependencies and retire the monolithic test
target.

a5a81b4b5abafacbb2c08321b7c7fc60276a9105 [ diff ] 
[Adaptive UI] Let a sheet's custom view supply its own vertical spacing

The sheet adds 16dp above the custom view and above the primary
button. A feature that passes its existing layout as CUSTOM_VIEW
already has its own spacing, so its sheet ends up taller than before.

Drop the margin above the custom view, and the primary button's top
margin when the custom view sits directly above it, the same way the
custom view already supplies its own side padding. Text above the
buttons keeps the 16dp gap, and so does the space below the buttons.
Dialogs don't change.

[ios][deflake] Fake turn on AutoFill prompt in password settings EG test

`testTapsOnTurnOnPasswordsInOtherAppsItem` flaked on slow iPad
simulators. The "Turn on AutoFill…" button is only re-enabled 2 seconds
after the system prompt completion handler runs, and the latency of
`ASSettingsHelper` is unbounded, so the 3 seconds wait was sometimes
too short.

- Fakes the `ASSettingsHelper` request in the test so that the
  completion handler runs right away and the re-enabling delay no longer
  depends on the system.
- Verified with 200/200 passing runs on ios-simulator-noncq
  (iPad (A16) iOS 27.0).

0466ff09b05d573c57b0ee8db6f5858253a6877f [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Add OmniboxIconSource

Icons identified by an IconResourceIds value (such as the AIM tool,
model and input icons in SearchboxConfig) need a surface-independent
way to be looked up and rendered at a given size and tint.

This adds the OmniboxIconSource interface and a process-wide source,
with a testing override. Keeping the interface separate from where the
icons come from lets surfaces adopt the source and test against it now.
For now the process-wide source knows no icons, so callers keep their
built-in icons.

Nothing uses it yet.

while WebUI content-setting support for these tests lands separately.

tree
895e1671183d9f378bdaae618347a8735384e125
parent
a135841efc80a5992f3020d2f5052d83c6fafe08 [ diff ] 
[tracing] Emit object count and size in native heap profiler samples

The org.chromium.native_heap_profiler data source only emitted each
sample's Poisson-scaled total bytes and dropped the allocation size.
CDP's sampling heap profile exposes both, which lets pprof converters
compute inuse_objects.

This CL emits two StackSample follower counters, declared once per
sequence in StackSampleDefaults.follower_descriptors:
- object_count (UNIT_COUNT): max(1, round(total / size)), as computed by
  heap_profiling::MergeSamples().
- object_size (UNIT_BYTES): the unscaled size of the sampled allocation.

Trace processor imports them as stack_sample_counter_track rows with
is_timebase = 0, and the Perfetto UI shows a flamegraph tab for each.

Make IsUserFacingUILocale take a base::i18n::LanguageTag

l10n_util::IsUserFacingUILocale() now accepts a const
base::i18n::LanguageTag& instead of std::string_view. This avoids
re-parsing tags in GetUserFacingUILocaleList(), which already iterates
over LanguageTags, and lets the matched locale be compared as a tag.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Record enterprise interstitial .repeat_visit decisions
📋 Metadata
commit
e89d9eb1378275aa610cf6ce680efff61e838fdd
[ log ]
author
Michał Kaczmarczyk <mickaczmarczyk@google.com>
Fri Nov 20 18:28:58 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Nov 20 18:28:58 2026
tree
010dd0faae69e9246dcda9b91a0f42a875ef9468
parent
ef387a283ef441ff3b1fa9cb0f7895f12c16715a [ diff ] 
Record enterprise interstitial .repeat_visit decisions

MetricsHelper only records `interstitial.<prefix>.decision.repeat_visit`
when it is given a HistoryService to look up prior visits to the host.
The enterprise warn and block interstitials passed nullptr on all
platforms, so `interstitial.enterprise_warn.decision.repeat_visit` and
`interstitial.enterprise_block.decision.repeat_visit` were never
recorded.

Pass the profile's HistoryService to MetricsHelper in
EnterpriseWarnControllerClient and EnterpriseBlockControllerClient
(Desktop/Android), and add an optional HistoryService parameter to
IOSBlockingPageMetricsHelper so the iOS enterprise interstitials can do
the same. Other iOS interstitials are unchanged.

Blocked navigations commit error pages, which are not added to history,
so the block histogram is only recorded when the host has prior visits,
e.g. from before the block policy took effect or through an allowed URL
on the same host.

[smart selection] Keep suggestions at the input box's leading edge

When the prompt is placed by its trailing edge (left of the selection,
or below/above with right edges aligned), the container used
align-items: flex-end. The searchbox row is wider than the chips row
when the dogfood feedback button shows, so the chips shifted past the
input box's leading edge. The container now always aligns its content to
the start; its position still comes from its measured width.

Recording: http://go/scrcast/NTA1NzEyMjg0Nzk0ODgwMHw1ZTkzYzVlZS02Mw

parent
b2d023572076e0cdc269b104e49c3deb61aebb78 [ diff ] 
Revert "[Mac] Wait for launch files/URLs in AppShimController for non-default launches"

This reverts commit 08e0bbb3329b5030c0c5454773005e6bfa7bd829.

Reason for revert: https://crbug.com/585883649

Original change's description:
> [Mac] Wait for launch files/URLs in AppShimController for non-default launches
>
> When an app shim is launched by macOS LaunchServices to open files or
> URLs, AppKit delivers the launch AppleEvents (kAEOpenDocuments /
> kAEGetURL) asynchronously around the initial launch sequence.
>
> Unlike -application:openFile: (for a single file), Apple documentation
> does not guarantee that -application:openURLs: will be called before
> -applicationDidFinishLaunching:. When LaunchServices delivers AppleEvents
> out-of-process, the AppleEvent can arrive 10-20ms after
> applicationDidFinishLaunching: has run.
>
> Previously, AppShimController::OnAppFinishedLaunching immediately
> connected to Chrome upon applicationDidFinishLaunching:. If the
> launch files/URLs had not yet arrived, OnShimConnected was sent with
> empty files and URLs, causing Chrome to treat it as a normal launch and
> open the app's start_url (e.g. basic.html). When the AppleEvent arrived
> shortly after, Chrome opened a second window for the file handler,
> leaving the spurious first window open and causing integration tests to
> time out waiting for the wrong window.
>
> This CL:
> 1. Inspects NSApplicationLaunchIsDefaultLaunchKey in
>    -[AppShimDelegate applicationDidFinishLaunching:]. Per Apple SDK
>    documentation, this key is NO when the app was launched to open or
>    print a file or URL, and YES for default launches.
> 2. When is_default_launch is false and no launch files or URLs have
>    been delivered yet, AppShimController enters kWaitingForLaunchFilesOrUrls
>    and waits for OpenFiles or OpenUrls to be called before connecting
>    to Chrome (with a 2-second safety fallback timeout).
> 3. When OpenFiles or OpenUrls arrives, it cancels the timeout and
>    proceeds to StartChromeConnection(), ensuring OnShimConnected includes
>    the launch files/URLs.
> 4. Accepts files and URLs into launch_files_ and launch_urls_ while
>    init_state_ < kHasSentOnShimConnected, appending incoming items.
> 5. Re-enables the previously filtered WebAppIntegration file handler test
>    in mac.mac26-arm64-rel.browser_tests.filter.
>
> TAG=agy
> CONV=f6caa073-1999-4de3-8e9f-eb0635550063
>
> Fixed: 548771734
> Change-Id: Ibd487c233ee0ea281c5b555eb8615b17edd9e762
> Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.mac:mac26-arm64-rel-tests|browser_tests
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8356327
> Commit-Queue: Nate Chapin <japhet@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: Marijn Kruisselbrink <mek@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1707483}

tree
a0cd14a5bc5c566e8447cbc1970af01c1cd0a9e2
parent
bbaad077c85dc61cc41a76674d91586ffbc53d6d [ diff ] 
infra: Switch android-cast-arm64 gtests from tangorpro to panther_on_14

try/android-cast-arm64-{dbg,rel} frequently hit INFRA_FAILURE due to
Swarming task expirations waiting for Pixel Tablet (tangorpro) devices.
The small tangorpro pool is heavily saturated by priority-25 CI traffic
from android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel (across main and active
milestone branches), starving try tasks at priority 30/29 even though
each Cast gtest only runs for ~15-18s.

The Cast ARM64 gtests do not require Pixel Tablet specifically—only an
Android 14 ARM64 device (see https://crrev.com/c/5892424). Switch
chromium_android_cast_receiver_arm64_gtests to panther_on_14 (Pixel 7,
Android 14 AP2A.240705.004), which has much larger capacity (~285 alive
bots, ~22% utilization, sub-second try pending times).

[BottomBar] Move preference change observer into bottom bar module

Activity recreation tracking for the bottom bar preference toggle
compiles inside the monolithic browser layer despite depending on
modularized lifecycle, preference, and bottom bar configuration
APIs. Keeping this observer outside the component scatters bottom
bar state management across separate packages and build targets.

Move the preference change observer and its unit tests into the
bottom bar UI module to consolidate toggle tracking with the rest of
the feature.

[DataSharing] Consolidate remaining unit tests into module test target

Four data sharing Robolectric test suites sit inside the data
sharing source tree yet still compile through a separate monolithic
test target in the top-level Android build graph. Splitting unit
tests for the same feature across two build definitions forces broad
test helper dependencies where modularized component dependencies
suffice.

Fold those remaining unit tests into the existing data sharing test
suite and retire the redundant monolithic test target.

[LHS] Preserve keyboard focus across rail toggles

https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTkyMjc5NDcxMjg1ODYyNHxjMDM3MjdiNC1hNg

Toggling the NTP threads rail marked the previous view inert without
moving focus, dropping keyboard focus to the document body on both open
and close.

Move focus into the expanded iframe container (or into the ghost loader
first, handing off once the iframe is ready without stealing external
focus), and expose the history accessible label on the iframe. When the
rail closes, return focus to the "Open sidebar" button.

[Agentic Suggestions][iOS] Prevent trailing blue dot from squashing

When the omnibox popup trailing button is first laid out with no icon
and later reused for the blue dot indicator (as happens on the first row
in AI Mode), UIButton shrinks its content rect vertically and squashes
the 8pt circle. Starting with no icon also left the button unhidden on
initialization, and changing trailing icon types did not invalidate
intrinsic content size.

Keep the button hidden when no icon is shown, invalidate intrinsic
content size on icon or trait changes, and center the dot image at its
natural size during layout.

IOS UI Screenshots:
- Before (AI Mode, first row dot squashed into an oval):
  https://screenshot.googleplex.com/uMYiJioQga9PNgX
- After (Standard omnibox, circular dots aligned with arrow):
  https://screenshot.googleplex.com/m6TpSVJ7JvYSpf3
- After (AI Mode, circular dot on first row aligned with arrow):
  https://screenshot.googleplex.com/fbVMMu6qmkPmhYF

dafe4427ef840ed0a11373593e7d90b7c0e46976 [ diff ] 
gardener: Increase components_unittests shards on win-asan to 8

This CL doubles the Swarming shard count for components_unittests on
win-asan (and its try mirror) from 4 to 8.

On Intel Broadwell GCE bots, win-asan components_unittests shards have
been taking 51.5 to 59.2 minutes and intermittently hitting Swarming's
60-minute timeout (e.g., builds 41060, 41062, and 41067), compared to
30.6 to 35.6 minutes on non-Broadwell bots. Doubling to 8 shards brings
worst-case shard runtime down to roughly 30 minutes.

0f1ff6cd28ad1549ac1eba84957155995c0e77aa
parent
4c32f71e9f9685b695e86eaf60159586b3d4fa94 [ diff ] 
🛡 [Omnibox] Limit direct loads of pasted text to HTTP(S) destinations

The "Paste and go to copied text" context menu item commits the
clipboard content in one step. AutocompleteMediator.loadPastedText()
loaded whatever destination the text classified to, on the assumption
that this is equivalent to typing the text and pressing Enter. It is
not: typed text is visible while it is committed, whereas the menu
label is static and the pasted text is first shown once the load is
already under way.

Only load the classified match right away when its destination is a
valid HTTP(S) URL, which matches the restriction applied to the
middle-click clipboard navigation paths on desktop. The destination
URL decides rather than the type of the match, so a search is loaded
because its results page is an HTTP(S) URL, not because it is a
search. For any other destination the text is handled as a regular
paste: it stays in the Omnibox, suggestions are requested for it, and
the user can commit it explicitly.

[ios][deflake] Fix testMoveItemThenRefreshSignIn sync and UI races

Fix two races causing testMoveItemThenRefreshSignIn() to flake on iPad
(especially in Debug builds on ios-fieldtrial-rel):

- Poll the fake sync server for the updated READ status of Page 1
  before calling signOut(). Checking entity count alone succeeds
  immediately from the initial unread upload while the READ commit is
  still in flight, causing signOut() to wipe account storage before the
  update reaches the server.
- Wait for both initial unread entries to upload before opening the
  Reading List, and mark Page 1 as read via edit mode rather than a 2s
  long-press context menu that collides with iPad table view
  drag-and-drop.

8d910410bf4a9a5d139715f82e80128eef35ac2e [ diff ] 
[tracing] Work around Perfetto's breakpad file lookup for Chrome IDs

Chrome records module IDs as 33-character hex strings, and
symbolize_trace names breakpad files '<module_id>.breakpad'. Since a
2023 Perfetto change, Perfetto's breakpad symbolizer hex-encodes the
module ID text before building the filename, so it looks for
'<hex of module_id text>.breakpad' (66 characters) and finds nothing.
symbolize_trace then reports success with no symbols added.

Until the fix reaches the pinned traceconv, point BREAKPAD_SYMBOL_DIR at
a temporary directory that links each breakpad file under both its
standard name and the name Perfetto looks up. The breakpad directory
itself, including --local_breakpad_dir caches, is left unchanged.

Also warn when no symbols were added to the trace.

      WebView heap profile with a 32-bit renderer breakpad file: 0 of
      403 renderer frames symbolized before, 403 of 403 after.

Test: vpython3 tools/tracing/symbolize_trace_unittest.py. Symbolized a
Bug: 586951445

Re-enable WebUI toolbar omnibox focus interactive tests

FullWebUIOmniboxInteractiveTest.FocusLocationBarAfterBlurSelectsAll was
skipped when WebUIToolbarEnabled is true in crrev.com/c/8741703 due to
focus flakiness [0], and FocusLocationCommandExitsKeywordMode was
skipped because WebUILocationBar restored the wrong text when exiting
keyword mode [1].

Since then, crrev.com/c/8833538 replaced the nested base::RunLoop in
WaitForPopupTransitionLockout() with a non-nested Kombucha StateObserver
[2], and crrev.com/c/8721872 updated
WebUILocationBar::HandleFocusRequestForFullPopup() to forward focus,
selection, and show_full_url directly via OmniboxPopupView::OnFocus()
when IsPopupHandlerReady() is true [3]. This lets
OmniboxPopupSearchboxElement::onSetFocus_() exit keyword mode cleanly
via clearKeyword() [4].

Remove both GTEST_SKIP() guards to re-enable these tests for WebUI
toolbar.

tree
184914630b08163bb047dc527617a8b98d1bb238
parent
6ee1626275778a5ce47b4d4dea84f90a680848e4 [ diff ] 
[Android] Avoid UpdateToolbarLayer on progress bar ticks

CompositorModelChangeProcessor always passes a null PropertyKey to
TopToolbarSceneLayer.bind(), causing it to unconditionally call
updateToolbarLayer() before updateProgressBar(). When the toolbar layer
is visible, native ToolbarLayer::PushResource() calls
ResourceManagerImpl::GetResource() for the dynamic toolbar resource,
triggering RequestResourceFromJava() and synchronous bitmap captures
when the toolbar view is dirty and unlocked (e.g., native pages,
same-document navigations, or post-load animations) as well as JNI and
capture-check overhead on every progress bar tick.

Guard behind disabled-by-default flag
TopToolbarSceneLayerSkipUpdateOnProgress and track dirty state
separately for toolbar-layer properties and PROGRESS_BAR_INFO via
PropertyModelChangeProcessor so progress-bar-only frames update only the
progress bar layers without invoking updateToolbarLayer() or
RequestResourceFromJava().

Fixed: b:587436445

Revert "[iOS] Move Next IA toolbar button state into ToolbarButtonsMediator"

This reverts commit 21d56e679d2c1301d4122ec21778fe13935686a2.

Reason for revert:
LUCI Bisection has identified this change as the culprit of a build failure. See the analysis: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/bisection/compile-analysis/b/8664593470247274929

Sample failed build: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664593470247274929

If this is a false positive, please report it at http://b.corp.google.com/createIssue?component=1199205&description=Analysis%3A+https%3A%2F%2Fchromium-review.googlesource.com%2Fc%2Fchromium%2Fsrc%2F%2B%2F8821895&format=PLAIN&priority=P3&title=Wrongly+blamed+https%3A%2F%2Fci.chromium.org%2Fui%2Fp%2Fchromium%2Fbisection%2Fcompile-analysis%2Fb%2F8664593470247274929&type=BUG

Original change's description:
> [iOS] Move Next IA toolbar button state into ToolbarButtonsMediator
>
> This CL moves the state of the Next IA toolbar's control buttons out of
> the per-toolbar ToolbarMediator into one ToolbarButtonsMediator per
> Browser, so that other surfaces can show the same buttons with the same
> state. No behavior change.
>
> - ToolbarButtonsConsumer: the button part of ToolbarConsumer, which now
>   extends it. ToolbarButtonsMutator: the button actions of
>   ToolbarMutator.
> - ToolbarButtonsMediator, in coordinator/buttons/: the web state,
>   navigation, actor control, tab count/group, menu and assistant parts
>   of ToolbarMediator. It computes the state and builds the menus once
>   per update, and only updates its active consumers. A consumer is
>   synced when it is added, which keeps the gate of crrev.com/c/8428167:
>   only the toolbar with the omnibox gets updates.
> - ToolbarMediator keeps the omnibox position, keyboard, banner promo and
>   the top toolbar's NTP visibility while it doesn't have the omnibox.
>   It tells MainToolbarCoordinator when its toolbar gets or loses the
>   omnibox, which adds it to or removes it from ToolbarButtonsMediator.
> - The side swipe snapshot only updates the snapshotted toolbar.
> - ToolbarViewController keeps its button taps and metrics for now, and
>   sends the button actions to ToolbarButtonsMediator. A follow-up moves
>   them to a helper shared by the surfaces.
>
> Bug: 585252907

277801a4425c5bda2d8bbc26ec0f012c1ad7f379 [ diff ] 
[CE] Check GetExecutionContext() before unregistering in RemovedFrom()

When a capability element is attached while its document is active,
`is_cache_registered_` is set to true. If the document's execution
context is subsequently destroyed (for example, when an enclosing iframe
is detached or navigated) before the capability element is removed from
the DOM, `GetExecutionContext()` returns nullptr during
`HTMLCapabilityElementBase::RemovedFrom()`, causing a null dereference
in `CachedPermissionStatus::From()`.

Fixed: 484088920

083b25e941c9025ca6bdd31a283ee8499c9559d4 [ diff ] 
[contextual-tasks] Move input state and context events out of mixin

Only the Contextual Tasks wrapper listens for `input-state-changed` and
`on-context-files-changed`, on the forked inner composebox, yet the
mixin dispatched both for every embedder. The fork now dispatches them
in `willUpdate()` and `updated()` under the same conditions instead.

Bug: b:517570350
